### 2026-09-04 16:48:33  #公司 #市場 #央行 #國際

【一眾資管巨頭開始重建黃金頭寸 看好其估值便宜與對沖價值】在金價回落後，全球部分規模最大的資產管理公司已重建黃金頭寸，押注即便美聯儲在抗擊通脹方面採取更強硬立場，支撐黃金的長期因素仍將持續。歐洲最大資產管理公司Amundi SA買入黃金，預計金價將在年底前重返每盎司5000美元。今年早些時候，金價從歷史高位回落後，瑞士百達資產管理、Robeco Institutional Asset Management BV和富達國際的基金經理也重新增持了此前削減的黃金頭寸。“我們認為黃金價格便宜，是一種良好的對沖工具，而且流動性也不錯，”Amundi投資研究所跨資產策略主管Lorenzo Portelli表示。不過他說，上月買入後，在考慮進一步增持之前，公司還需要更清楚地瞭解美聯儲的利率路徑。這一觀點在對十多位資產管理人士的採訪中相當普遍，他們所在公司的資產管理規模合計達27萬億美元。所有受訪者無一例外，包括法巴資產管理和Manulife John Hancock Investments，最近幾周不是重新增持了黃金，就是維持看漲配置。不過，多位資產管理人士表示，金價要突破近期每盎司約4600美元的上限，過程不會一帆風順。美國國債收益率上升，以及市場加大押注美聯儲年底前至少加息一次，都在削弱對黃金的支撐。由於黃金本身不產生利息，在借貸成本上升時通常吸引力會下降。

### 2026-09-04 16:43:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Slovenia Trade Gap Widens to Nearly 2-Year High — Slovenia recorded a trade deficit of EUR 1,580.3 million in July 2026, shifting from a surplus of EUR 479.1 million in the same month a year earlier. This marked the largest shortfall since October 2024, as import growth far outpaced exports. Imports surged 49.1% year-on-year to EUR 8,297.5 million, driven largely by increased purchases from both EU (+5.4%) and non-EU countries (+94.0%). Meanwhile, exports rose 11.3% to EUR 6,717.2 million, lifted by higher shipments to both EU (+8.6%) and non-EU markets (+13.6%). For the January–July period, Slovenia recorded a trade deficit of EUR 5,693.2 million, shifting from a surplus of EUR 3,681.6 million in the same period of the previous year, weighed down by a 6.2% drop in exports, compared with an 11.2% increase in imports.

### 2026-09-04 16:42:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Construction PMI #Importance 1

UK Construction Activity Drops More than Anticipated — The S&P Global UK Construction PMI eased to 44.3 in August of 2026 from 44.7 in the previous month, missing expectations of 45.5. Survey respondents noted fresh weakness in demand conditions from clients that caused a drop in new projects, especially in residential construction starts, amid lower risk sentiment as the war in the Middle East raises both energy prices and borrowing costs. The residential work index slumped to 37.6, while civil engineering activity dropped to 40.5 and commercial construction activity fell to 47.8. Consequently, aggregate construction firms reduced head counts. Input cost inflation picked up on larger transportation bills, raw material prices, and fuel costs. Looking ahead, companies were still upbeat for the year ahead due to expectations of new tender opportunities.

Taiwan Forex Reserves Edge Higher — Taiwan's foreign exchange reserves rose to USD 601.9 billion in July 2026 from USD 594.27 billion in the previous month. The increase was mainly due to returns from the investment and utilization of foreign exchange reserves, exchange rate fluctuations of major currencies against the US sollar. Additionally, the increase in reserves was also due to central bank operations aimed at maintaining stability in the foreign exchange market. Meanwhile, foreign investors' holdings of domestic stocks and bonds, valued at market prices, along with their New Taiwan Dollar deposits, totaled USD 1,861 billion, equivalent to approximately 309% of total foreign exchange reserves.

UK New Car Registrations Rise for 9th Month — New car registrations in the UK rose 13.7% from the previous year to 94,236 units in August of 2026, picking up from the 11.7% rise in the earlier month, according to the Society of Motor Manufacturers and Traders (SMMT). It was a ninth consecutive period of growth, extending the market's recovery toward pre-pandemic levels. Plug-in hybrid electric vehicles registrations soared by 39.8% to 13,707, and battery electric vehicles rose by 27.7% to 28,063. In turn, hybrid electric vehicles rose by 26.3% to 11,940. These were enough to offset the 2.5% decline in petrol vehicles, which totaled 36,048.

### 2026-09-04 16:24:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Business Confidence #Importance 0

Philippine Business Confidence Turns Pessimistic — The Business Confidence Index in the Philippines turned negative at -20.3 in July 2026 from 0.0 in June, as firms grew more concerned about higher oil prices, renewed tensions in the Middle East, and persistent inflation. Current-month indicators weakened, with business activity falling to -0.1 from 8.3 in June, total orders to -0.7 from 5.4, and access to credit to -7.0 from -5.7. Financial conditions also deteriorated to -31.4 from -26.8, while capacity utilization fell to 68.6% from 73.9%. Meanwhile, three-month confidence eased to 3.7 from 18.8, while 12-month confidence declined to 29.4 from 42.4. Firms also lowered expectations for activity and orders, while fewer planned to hire amid softer growth. However, the expansion plans index rose to 20.8 from 18.7, signaling that more firms planned to expand next year. Twelve-month inflation expectations rose to 50.1 from 35.7.

### 2026-09-04 16:23:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #World #Food Price Index #Importance 2

World Food Prices Highest Since 2022 — The FAO Food Price Index rose 1.9% to 133.3 points in August 2026, marking its second consecutive monthly increase and reaching its highest level since November 2022. The rise reflected broad-based increases across all commodity categories. Sugar prices recorded the biggest increase, jumping 11.9% to a new high since June 2025, amid growing concerns over the global sugar supply outlook in the 2026/27 season. Dairy prices went up 2.3%, driven by higher milk powder and cheese. Cereals rose 2.2% to May 2024-highs, supported by robust demand, weather-related concerns over crop prospects in key producing regions, and continued uncertainty surrounding Black Sea export flows. Meat cost increased 1% reflecting higher poultry, pig and ovine meat prices. Finally, cost for vegetable oils edged up 0.6% to its highest level since June 2022, amid higher world palm and soy oil prices, which more than offset lower quotations for sunflower and rapeseed oils.

### 2026-09-04 16:21:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 1

Italian Retail Sales Drop for 2nd Month — The value of retail sales in Italy decreased 0.4% month-on-month in July 2026, following a revised 0.2% fall in June and against market forecasts for a 0.2% increase. It marked the second consecutive monthly decline, amid lower sales of both food (-0.4% vs -0.6% in June) and non-food products (-0.4% vs 0.2%). On an annual basis, retail sales went up 0.8%, the smallest increase since September 2025, decelerating from a 3.1% rise in the previous month, as growth slowed for both food (0.3% vs 1.9%) and non-food (1.1% vs 4.0%) sales. Among non-food products, notable gains were recorded in sales of household appliances, radios, televisions, and tape recorders (6.1%) and other products (5.0%), though these were partly offset by a decline in footwear, leather goods, and travel goods (-2.8%). By distribution channel, retail sales increased at large retail outlets (1.6%) and in online commerce (2.4%).

### 2026-09-04 16:15:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Pound Extends Gains as Markets Await US Jobs Data — The British pound extended gains above $1.35 on Friday, rebounding from two-week lows as investors awaited fresh direction from US employment data due later in the day while continuing to digest signals from major central banks. Bank of England Chief Economist Huw Pill said on Thursday that raising interest rates now could reduce the risk of the central bank having to tighten policy more aggressively later to contain inflation, which has risen amid the fallout from the Iran war. Markets are now fully pricing in a BoE rate hike by year-end, with another increase expected by March 2027. Meanwhile, Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller said recent data showed signs of disinflation and that, if upcoming figures confirmed the trend, he would favor keeping interest rates unchanged at this month’s policy meeting. Markets responded swiftly, with futures pricing in roughly a 50% chance of a Fed rate hike this month, down from around 63% a day earlier.

### 2026-09-04 16:08:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Gains for 2nd Day — The DAX 40 edged up 0.3% to around 26,070 on Friday after a slow start, extending modest gains for a second session. The automotive sector provided the main support, led by a 7% surge in Volkswagen shares following the board’s approval of its biggest restructuring plan in 89 years. The plan includes 50,000 job cuts and alternatives for four German plants, while also reducing the supervisory board’s influence on key decisions, where unions and Lower Saxony hold a majority. BMW and Volkswagen rose 1.7% and 1.1%, respectively. Other notable performers included Heidelberg Materials (2.3%), Infineon Technologies (1.5%) and Airbus (1.2%). Meanwhile, market participants continued to assess the outlook for the monetary policy, with the ECB expected to deliver a 25 bps rate hike next week. Developments in the Middle East also remained in focus. The index was set to decline about 1.9% this week.

FTSE MIB Underperforms — The FTSE MIB fell to around 52,000 on Friday, giving back some gains from the previous session and underperforming its regional peers, as losses among heavyweight financials weighed on the index. Shares of Monte dei Paschi di Siena fell around 0.6% after the lender received ECB approval to merge recently acquired Mediobanca (-0.6%) into the group, with investors weighing the integration of the two banks. Banca Generali also declined 0.2% despite reporting its best-ever net inflows for both August and the first eight months of the year. Other laggards included UniCredit (-1.6%), Intesa Sanpaolo (-0.5%), Banco BPM (-1.7%) and BPER Banca (-0.9%). On the flipside, technology stocks outperformed, with STMicroelectronics and Prysmian gaining 1.9% and 1.1%, respectively. On the data front, Italy’s retail sales unexpectedly fell 0.4% month-on-month in July, defying expectations for a 0.2% increase. For the week, the benchmark index is heading for a fourth consecutive decline.

### 2026-09-04 16:06:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Aluminum at Over 3-Week High — Aluminum futures in the UK hovered around $3,310 per tonne, an over three-week high, amid continued supply tightness and declining inventories. The latest escalation in US-Iran hostilities has raised uncertainty over the potential return of aluminum supplies from the Persian Gulf. This came as LME inventories remained near a 36-year low, while SHFE inventories fell 3% from the previous week, underscoring tight conditions in the physical market. Meanwhile, the Alunorte plant in Brazil, the world’s largest alumina refinery outside China, temporarily cut production to 50% of capacity in August, though output was subsequently ramped back up to full capacity following a temporary terminal access agreement with its gas supplier. Some of the supply pressures could also be partly eased by planned capacity restarts and expansion projects among several producers, as well as increased Chinese exports, with shipments from the world’s largest producer rising 18.7% year-on-year in July.

### 2026-09-04 15:47:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Construction PMI #Importance 1

Italy Construction Activity Falls to 4-Year Low — The S&P Global Italy Construction PMI fell to 41.7 in August 2026 from 49.1 in the previous month, marking its fastest fall since August 2022, as construction activity declined sharply amid a solid drop in new work. The downturn was concentrated in housing and commercial construction, while civil engineering activity fell only modestly. New orders declined for the fifth time in six months, with firms citing weaker demand, geopolitical uncertainty, permit delays, staff shortages, and the end of the National Recovery and Resilience Plan (PNRR). Purchasing activity fell at its fastest pace in over three and a half years, while supply-chain pressures intensified. Meanwhile, cost inflation eased to its softest pace since the start of the year. Employment continued to rise slightly, but subcontractor use fell sharply. Business confidence returned to positive territory, supported by strong order pipelines, though concerns over the PNRR's end and weaker investment persisted.

### 2026-09-04 15:45:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Slovakia GDP Growth Revises Slightly Higher — Slovakia’s economy grew by 0.8% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, slightly above the preliminary estimate but edging down from a 0.9% expansion in Q1. The largest positive contribution came from the wholesale and retail trade, transport, accommodation, and food service sectors, expanding 2.3% and accounting for 21.8% of total gross value added. Industry, representing 17.5% of GDP, recorded more modest growth of 0.3%. On the expenditure side, domestic demand was the main driver, with government consumption returning to growth (1.7% vs -0.4%), while household spending eased (0.6% vs 1.1%). Gross fixed capital formation fell 0.3%. Meanwhile, exports of goods and services increased 3.0%, while imports rose 3.7%, with the foreign trade balance reaching a surplus of more than EUR 0.5 billion, its highest positive value since the first quarter of 2024. On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, the economy expanded 0.2%, matching the pace recorded in the previous quarter.

French Construction Sector Suffers Setback — The S&P Global France Construction PMI slipped to 37.3 in August 2026 from 41.5 in July, signalling a faster contraction in construction activity across France, the sharpest since May 2020. New orders continued to fall, posting their fastest decline in four months and extending the downturn since April 2022. Firms cut jobs for the 28th month running, with the pace of reductions accelerating. Purchasing volumes declined as well, though the fall was the weakest in six months. Meanwhile, input cost inflation remained well above its survey average and picked up for the first time since April. Supply chain conditions remained challenging, but delivery delays eased to their lowest in six months. Lastly, French constructors grew more pessimistic about the year ahead, with expectations for business activity falling to their lowest since April.

However, civil engineering returned to contraction after a slight increase in July, while new orders continued to fall as high prices and borrowing costs weighed on demand. Employment declined for a seventh consecutive month, with job losses accelerating to their fastest pace since April. ### 2026-09-04 15:15:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Unexpectedly Cuts Rates by 50 Bps to 16.25% — The National Bank of Kazakhstan (NBK) lowered its base rate by 50 bps to 16.25% at its September 2026 meeting, surprising markets that had widely expected policymakers to cut borrowing costs by 25 bps. The decision followed July’s 25 bps cut, as annual inflation eased for an eleventh consecutive month to 9.8% in August, marking the lowest reading since February 2025. The central bank has lowered rates by a total of 175 bps since June, bringing the policy rate to its lowest level since February 2025. However, the NBK noted that the external environment remains unstable amid high energy prices due to the ongoing conflict in the Middle East and increasing external inflationary pressures. The central bank revised its inflation forecast for 2027 to 6.5%-8.5% from 5.5%-7.5% previously due to rising external inflation and an increase in fiscal stimulus. “However, given intensifying inflationary factors and risks, the scope for further cuts is limited,” the regulator said.

Grain names recovered from an early dip; Yasheng Group and Dunhuang Seed hit limit-up. Maritime equipment outperformed with China shipbuilding-related stocks leading; China Shipbuilding rose over 9%. Liquor stocks rallied on a yellow-wine theme; Guyue Longshan and Kuaijishan hit limit-up. Other notable gainers included tourism & hotels, beverages & food, digital currency, retail, film, building materials & cement, and AI applications. Technology stocks broadly pulled back; memory chips, MLCC, CPO, helium, lithography equipment, PCB, liquid-cooled servers and humanoid-robot names were among the worst performers. Turnover across Shanghai and Shenzhen reached 2.03 tln yuan, up roughly 271.8 bln yuan vs. the previous session; about 2,200 stocks advanced and 2,800 declined.

【German bond yields set for fourth straight weekly rise】Germany's government bond yields are set to rise for a fourth straight week, the longest run since mid-July, as investors price central banks keeping policy restrictive amid resilient growth and persistent inflation. Renewed US-Iran hostilities have lifted Middle East supply risk and pushed oil toward its biggest weekly gain since mid-July. Germany's 10-year bund yield is up about 7.5 bps this week; the 2-year yield is up about 7 bps, its largest weekly rise since mid-July. Traders price the ECB deposit rate at 2.73% by December, implying roughly a 90% probability of another hike after September, and expect the policy rate to reach 3% by September next year.

【China, Germany hold 10th electric vehicle cooperation steering committee meeting】The 10th China‑Germany Electric Vehicle Cooperation Steering Committee met online on Sept 1, 2026. Co‑chaired by the deputy director of the International Cooperation Department at China’s Ministry of Science and Technology and the director‑general for policy affairs at Germany’s Federal Ministry of Transport, the meeting reviewed EV development and policy trends, assessed progress of the Sino‑German EV Innovation Support Center (SGEC) and discussed future cooperation. Parties agreed to deepen technology‑innovation cooperation in the electric‑vehicle sector to support a green, low‑carbon transition. Participants included officials from Chinese and German transport and science ministries, China Automotive Technology & Research Center, Beijing Institute of Technology, and automakers BYD and Chery, along with German cooperation agencies.

【日本央行會議前夕，日元期權處於2024年以來最高溢價水平】交易員押注日本央行或將釋放重要前瞻指引，針對下一次日本央行政策決議的日元對沖成本達到1月以來最高。週三早盤，美元兌日元兩週期隱含波動率報5.97%；上週五該指標一度衝高至12.86%，為2025年6月以來第三高位。該期限期權的期限溢價達到591個基點，期權價格創下2024年11月美國大選之前以來的最高水平。日本央行將於9月18日公佈決議；市場定價已計入25個基點加息預期，但核心關注點在於央行是否釋放持續加息的明確訊號。該兩週期限同時覆蓋9月16日美聯儲議息會議，此次會議懸念十足，市場認為美聯儲加息機率近乎五五開。該期限下美元兌日元風險逆轉指標此前擴大至309個基點，看跌期權溢價高於看漲期權，此後這一行情動能消退。

【Cuba issues measures to boost foreign investment, trade and tourism】Cuba on the 3rd issued measures to ease restrictions on foreign investment, foreign trade and tourism. Key changes: remove requirement that foreign and joint-venture employers hire Cuban staff via intermediaries authorized by the Ministry of Labor and Social Security and permit direct hiring; replace prior Cuban central bank approval for joint ventures and domestic investors to open overseas bank accounts with post-facto reporting to the central bank; update import-export procedures to allow private firms to engage directly in foreign trade; relax limits on foreign capital participation in wholesale and retail and in priority and historical-cultural investment zones; introduce tax incentives for eco‑tourism projects; and allow foreign firms, foreign capital and non‑state operators to participate in tourist transport and travel agency businesses.

【古巴出臺政策進一步促進外資、外貿和旅遊業發展】古巴政府3日出臺一批新政策，進一步減少對外國投資、對外貿易和旅遊業的限制，擴大外資、合資和私營企業等的經營空間。古巴政府當日公佈的新政策包括：取消外資、合資企業必須透過古巴勞動和社會保障部授權的中介機構僱用古巴員工的要求，允許直接僱用；取消合資企業和參與國際經濟合作的本國投資者在境外開立銀行賬戶須事先獲得古巴央行批准的要求，改為事後向央行報備；更新貨物進出口程式，允許私營企業直接開展對外貿易；放寬外國資本參與批發、零售貿易以及重點區域和歷史文化遺產區域投資的限制；針對生態旅遊專案推出稅收激勵措施，並允許外國企業、外資及非國有經營主體參與旅遊運輸和旅行社業務等。（新華社）

ASX 200 Slides Near 1% for Week — The ASX 200 slipped 14 points, or 0.2%, to finish at 9,006 on Friday, reversing modest gains in early deals and ending the week down almost 1%, as stronger-than-expected Australia's GDP readings in Q2 revived fears of another rate hike by the Reserve Bank later this month. Sentiment also turned cautious ahead of key data due next week in top trading partner China, notably August CPI and PPI, as well as trade performance. Meanwhile, traders were on edge as the U.S. payrolls report looms large. Losses in energy minerals, utilities, and industrials weighed, though strength in non-energy miners, tech, and healthcare helped limit declines. Energy names lagged, with Ampol tumbling 5.5% while Woodside (-1.4%) and Santos (-0.7%) also fell. Heavyweight BHP Group (-2.2%), Computershare (-2.9%), PLS (-2.3%), and Xero (-1.7%) also retreated. In contrast, financials provided support, with the big four banks posting modest gains, while select tech stocks advanced.

【光大期貨：成本鋼招雙重引爆，矽鐵漲停創近兩年新高】週五，矽鐵漲幅領先一眾黑色系品種。主力合約早盤觸及漲停，隨後開啟，截至11:30收盤再度封板，創2024年10月以來新高。此前一個交易日，矽鐵主力合約已收漲2%以上，單日增倉2萬餘手，資金參與熱情明顯提升。蘭炭是本輪上漲的“第一推手”。據Mysteel資料，蘭炭原料紅柳林塊煤9月3日成交均價為1184元/噸，較上期均價大漲288元/噸。本週蘭炭小料價格環比上漲約230元/噸。8月蘭炭已完成三輪提漲，矽鐵生產成本累計抬升約300元/噸。與此同時，矽鐵成本“三件套”——電力、蘭炭、化工焦——全線走強。主產區電價維持高位，化工焦價格上行。矽鐵是高耗能行業，電費在成本中佔比最大，電價一漲成本底就隨之抬高。成本抬升的直接結果是產區利潤被壓縮至極限。在虧損壓力下，廠家挺價意願強烈，低價貨源難尋。需求方面，進入9月，主流鋼廠新一輪矽鐵招標密集啟動。寶鋼9月1日釋出招標公告，計劃採購FESI75AL1.5-B型號矽鐵1580噸；江西某鋼廠9月矽鐵招標定價6450元/噸（600噸，承兌到廠）；江蘇某鋼廠9月矽鐵定價6400元/噸（600噸，現金含稅到廠）。主流鋼廠招標價普遍開至6400-6450元/噸。9月鋼廠招標“量價齊升”，點燃需求預期。（光大期貨）

UK Natural Gas Prices Rise Amid Middle East Tensions — UK natural gas prices rose to 179 pence per therm on Friday, recovering some of the previous session’s losses as investors assessed renewed tensions in the Middle East. The latest flare-up came after a US attack on Iran triggered retaliatory strikes on American bases across the region, sending European gas prices to their highest level in more than three years. Although President Trump indicated that renewed strikes on Tehran would not last too long, tensions remain high around the Strait of Hormuz. The shipping paralysis continues to hamper Gulf LNG supplies to Europe, slowing the pace of inventory replenishment. Europe’s gas inventories for the upcoming winter remain abnormally low, raising the risk of tighter competition and leaving the region vulnerable to price spikes during colder months. Despite the recent decline, UK gas prices have climbed more than 8% this week, their fourth consecutive weekly gain.

### 2026-09-04 14:44:39  #央行 #市場 #國際

【將86噸黃金“運出”北美！荷蘭央行回應：一旦出現危機，方便快速動用】當地時間9月2日，荷蘭中央銀行宣佈了一項近年來規模罕見的黃金儲備調整：今年3月至8月，該行將約86噸黃金儲備從美國紐約和加拿大渥太華轉移至英國倫敦。調整完成後，荷蘭黃金儲備存放在紐約的比例從31.3%降至18.5%，加拿大渥太華的比例從19.7%降至18.5%；相比之下，倫敦的比例則從18.1%躍升至32.1%，成為荷蘭海外黃金儲備最主要的存放地。為什麼選擇現在調整？為什麼是倫敦？對此，荷蘭央行向記者指出，此次調整的核心目標，是在日益不確定的全球環境下，進一步加強危機應對能力。“從可售性與流動性的角度來看，倫敦具備額外優勢。”荷蘭央行表示，將黃金存放在倫敦，在危機情況下，有助於實現更快速、更高效的交易和調動。(每日經濟新聞)

### 2026-09-04 14:43:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Hungary Retail Sales Hit 4-Month High — Retail sales in Hungary rose 4.9% year-on-year in July 2026, picking up from a seven-month low of 3% in the previous month. It marked the strongest annual growth since March, driven by faster increases in sales of food, beverages and tobacco (2.2% vs 1.8% in June) and non-food products (6.5% vs 5.1%). Within non-food items, sales accelerated at pharmaceutical, medical product and perfume stores (9.2%), book, computer and other industrial goods (5.9%), textile, clothing and footwear (5.4%), furniture and technical goods (5.2%), and industrial goods-type mixed (5.1%). Retail activity was also supported by a sharp rebound in fuel sales (8.1% vs -0.3%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail activity rose 1%, recovering from a 0.4% decline in June.

### 2026-09-04 14:42:30  #MKT News #Market Themes #China EV Leaders #CSI300 #Impact bearish #Hang Seng Index #Copper

【CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu: China's auto industry in 1H undergoing deep transformation and fierce competition for existing market】CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu says in 1H the Chinese auto industry is in deep transformation and intense competition for existing demand as NEV penetration rises. The sector shows revenue growth without profit and widening divergence: domestic OEMs still growing top line but at a slower pace and remain smaller than global peers; profits have concentrated with upstream battery leaders while downstream dealers face a severe survival crisis. Price wars and high inventories are intensifying cash-flow stress and supply-chain payment pressures, raising systemic risk. Cui notes a multi-dimensional deterioration across revenue, gross margin, expenses, net profit, inventory and cash flow, and highlights that overseas sales—where volumes and margins are higher—are the most important route to relief for most automakers.

Iron Ore Set for Second Weekly Gain — Iron ore futures climbed toward CNY 730 per ton and were on track for a second consecutive weekly gain, supported by elevated ocean freight costs and expectations of pre-holiday restocking in top buyer China. Industry data also showed that iron ore inventories at major Chinese ports declined in the latest week, marking a fourth consecutive weekly drop and signaling potential for restocking. Elsewhere, Brazilian miner Usiminas temporarily suspended operations at its Samambaia iron ore plant in Itatiaiuçu from September 2, citing weaker ore prices and sharply higher freight costs. Meanwhile, iron ore prices may face a ceiling as margins at Chinese steel mills continue to shrink.

### 2026-09-04 14:27:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Current Account #Importance 1

Sweden Posts Largest Current Account Surplus in Over a Year — Sweden’s current account surplus widened to SEK 126.2 billion in the second quarter of 2026, from SEK 103.7 billion in the corresponding period a year earlier. This marked the largest surplus since the first quarter of 2025, driven by a wider primary income surplus, which rose to SEK 100.9 billion from SEK 76.0 billion, reflecting higher investment returns (SEK 97.6 billion vs SEK 72.5 billion in Q2 2025). The services deficit narrowed to SEK 14.0 billion from SEK 36.6 billion, as exports rose by SEK 31 billion, far outpacing the SEK 8.4 billion increase in imports. On the other hand, the goods surplus narrowed to SEK 67.7 billion from SEK 86.5 billion, as imports increased by SEK 61.5 billion, exceeding the SEK 42.7 billion rise in exports. The secondary income deficit also widened to SEK 28.4 billion from SEK 22.3 billion.

【超強厄爾尼諾來襲 分析師警告全球糧食危機逼近】今年的厄爾尼諾現象或將成為至少70年來最強的一次，這進一步加大了全球農產品市場受到大範圍干擾的風險。聯合國下屬機構世界氣象組織（WMO）9月3日釋出最新通報，確認厄爾尼諾現象已形成並將持續增強，預計將發展為超強級別事件，對全球降水和溫度格局產生重大影響，相關極端天氣風險將持續至2027年。“厄爾尼諾的影響已經實實在在地顯現出來了。”荷蘭合作銀行(Rabobank)的分析師在一份報告中寫道。這些分析師表示，對厄爾尼諾影響的擔憂正助推一系列大宗商品價格走高。巴克萊分析師在一份報告中寫道，今年厄爾尼諾現象異常強勁，可能會帶來進一步的擾亂。他們表示：“從歷史來看，厄爾尼諾事件往往與天氣敏感型市場的劇烈波動有關。”滙豐銀行分析師上週在一份報告中敲響警鐘，指出穀物供應趨緊、厄爾尼諾現象、海上航運要道受阻，以及由此引發的供應擠壓，正推高穀物價格。

### 2026-09-04 14:16:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Romania Retail Sales Fall at Softer Pace — Romania’s retail sales fell 6.1% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the twelfth consecutive month of decline, but easing from an upwardly revised 7.4% drop in the previous month. The softer contraction was broad-based, with sales falling less sharply in food, beverages and tobacco (-5.8% vs -7.3% in June), non-food products (-6.7% vs -6.8%), and automotive fuel in specialized stores (-4.4% vs -5.8%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade rose 0.8%, rebounding from an upwardly revised 1.3% drop in June. In the first seven months of the year, retail sales declined 5.8% from the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-09-04 14:14:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Factory Orders #Importance 2

German Factory Orders Rise More than Estimated — Germany's factory orders rose 2.5% mom in July 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 3.7% gain in the previous month but outperforming market expectations of 0.3%. It was the third straight month of increase, mainly driven by large orders for shipbuilding, rail vehicle construction, and aircraft manufacturing (126.4%). Meanwhile, bookings for the automotive industry declined 12.5%. Capital goods (2.4%) and intermediate goods (4.3%) rose, while consumer goods slipped (-4.8%). Domestic demand surged (9.1%), while foreign orders dropped 2.1%, dragged by a 10.1% fall in orders from non-euro area countries that offset a 12.1% surge from the euro area. Excluding large-scale contracts, orders were 1.4% lower. On a less volatile three-month basis, demand increased 2.9% in May-July from the prior three months, though they dipped 2.2% when large orders were excluded.

【世界氣象組織冰凍圈專家：冰川消融加速，高亞洲地區風險尤其值得關注】近日，世界氣象組織（WMO）冰凍圈監測和預測服務負責人、冰凍圈專家網路組協調人範德韋爾（Narelle Van Der Wel）博士就此次泥石流災害的嚴重性、早期預警、國際合作等相關話題接受了專訪。他表示，我們無法對全球風險進行精確排序，因為風險不僅受冰川變化影響，還取決於承災體暴露程度、脆弱性以及防災準備水平。但是，高亞洲地區（HMA）尤其值得關注：一方面當地冰凍圈正發生快速變化，另一方面大量人口居住在可能遭受這類災害的區域，道路、水電站、居民點與跨境貿易通道也在不斷擴張。該區域地形異常陡峭、地勢高差懸殊，冰、岩石、沉積物與洪水可以快速遠距離運動，進一步放大了級聯災害的潛在破壞力。（第一財經）

Estonia Industrial Output Growth Hits 6-Month High — Industrial production in Estonia increased by 2.3% year-on-year in July 2026, recovering from a 4.6% decline in the previous month and marking its highest level since January. Output rebounded in manufacturing (1.1% vs -5.3% in June), supported by gains in the manufacture of food (1.5%), beverages (5.0%), wearing apparel (11.4%), leather and related products (41.7%), wood and wood products (10.8%), basic pharmaceutical products and pharmaceutical preparations (162.3%), other non-metallic mineral products (8.9%), computer, electronic and optical products (3.0%), machinery and equipment n.e.c. (30.3%), and furniture (3.2%). Electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply also recovered (16.0% vs -12.6%). Meanwhile, mining and quarrying growth slowed (13.5% vs 26.7%). On a monthly basis, industrial activity grew by 0.9% in July, rebounding from a 2.3% decline in the previous month.

NZX 50 Ends 0.9% Higher, Posts Weekly Gain — New Zealand stocks rose 128 points, or 0.9%, to 13,974 on Friday, halting losses in the previous session, amid signals that the RBNZ will hold interest rates at its October meeting after raising them in July and September. New Zealand’s central bank is more likely to wait until December before raising interest rates again, according to Assistant Governor Karen Silk. The broader index tracked an upbeat session on Wall Street on Thursday amid easing Treasury yields. However, traders anticipated US jobless rate data due later today, China’s consumer and producer data, as well as trade figures for August due next week. Consumer discretionary, tech, financials, real estate, and healthcare mainly drove the index, with top movers including Hallenstein Glasson (6.3%), Gentrack Group (3.8%), Mainfreight (3.9%), Channel Infrastructure (2.3%), Fisher & Paykel (1.2%), Freightways Group (0.9%), and Westpac Banking Corp. (0.8%). For the week, the index rose 1.5%, after falling in the previous week.

【梅洛尼破二戰後義大利連續執政時間紀錄】9月4日是義大利總理梅洛尼連續執政第1413天，她由此成為第二次世界大戰結束以來義大利連續執政時間最長的總理。二戰後義大利連續執政時間最長的紀錄此前由已故前總理貝盧斯科尼保持，為1412天。雖然貝盧斯科尼連續執政時長被梅洛尼超越，他依然是二戰後義大利任職時間最長的總理。據統計，1994年至2011年，貝盧斯科尼四次執政，累計時長約9年。梅洛尼2022年10月就職。義大利黨派林立，政府更迭頻繁。據統計，二戰結束後，義大利經歷了近70次政府更迭。但過去幾年，對比數度重新組閣的法國、數次更換首相的英國，義大利政局相對穩定。不過，近期民意調查結果顯示，2027年議會選舉後，義大利可能將出現更趨分裂且不穩定的政治格局。（央視新聞）

【Sichuan launches new grain-capacity drive, guarantees 96 mln mu sown area; subsidies, price support and insurance expanded】Sichuan's agricultural modernization plan pledges to keep grain sown area at or above 96 mln mu (≈6.4 mln ha) and launches a new round of grain-capacity enhancement. The plan deepens the Tianfu Granary — 100 counties, 1,000 plots program and promotes 'ton-per-mu' field construction, integrating the 'four goods' (good land, good seed, good machinery, good methods) to raise yields at scale. It calls for optimizing crop mix and regional layout: boost rice and corn capacity, stabilize wheat, consolidate soybean and oilseed expansion gains, and develop autumn–winter potatoes and ratoon rice where suitable. Policy measures include provincial fiscal subsidies for large-scale grain growers, grain production incentives, continuation of the minimum purchase price for paddy, and wider rollout of full-cost insurance for the three major grains plus full-cost soybean insurance and planting-income insurance.

Rupiah Set for Fifth Weekly Rise as Dollar Retreats — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,650 per U.S. dollar on Friday, remaining below IDR 17,700 for a second straight session as the dollar weakened further after traders scaled back expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month following dovish comments from a central bank official. Meanwhile, Bank Indonesia Governor Destry Damayanti said domestic inflation remains under control and called for closer cooperation among the government, central bank, businesses, and economists to support economic growth. Gains were tempered, however, by growing concerns that El Niño could disrupt food supplies and push up prices in the coming months. Market caution also prevailed ahead of next week’s data releases, including August foreign exchange reserves, consumer confidence, and July retail sales, which could offer fresh clues on domestic demand and monetary policy. Still, the rupiah was on track to strengthen for a fifth straight week, gaining around 0.25% so far.

### 2026-09-04 12:20:25  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #US10Y #Impact bearish

【Norway sovereign wealth fund proposes about $80 bln cut to US Treasuries】Norges Bank Investment Management (NBIM), which manages Norway’s $2.3 tln sovereign wealth fund, proposed to the finance ministry on Tuesday lowering the government-debt weight in the fund’s benchmark bond index from 70% to 50%. NBIM estimates the change would reduce the fund’s global government bond allocation by roughly $106 bln, largely via cuts to US Treasuries. The manager proposes cutting US Treasury exposure by 12.2 percentage points while increasing US non-government fixed-income exposure by 11.4 percentage points—actions NBIM says would lower US Treasury holdings by about $80 bln. The fund’s overall fixed-income allocation is just under 26% of assets. Allocation to UK government bonds would remain unchanged; exposure to Japanese government bonds would rise by 2.8 percentage points.

【China market regulator opens seven cases targeting barriers to fair market access】On Sept. 4 the State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) announced a second batch of seven enforcement cases targeting obstacles to fair market entry across China’s healthcare, energy, funeral services, waste collection and internet-access sectors. SAMR said violations fell into three types: discriminatory entry requirements creating regional barriers; limits on counterparties or exclusive cooperation agreements that produce de facto exclusive operators; and failure to implement policy adjustments or delayed approvals that hinder market entry. South Korean Won Rises to Over 1-Year High — The South Korean won traded around 1,355 per dollar, extending gains to its strongest level since early July 2025, as easing expectations for another US rate hike weakened the dollar and lowered Treasury yields. Fed Governor Christopher Waller indicated support for keeping rates steady if inflation continues to cool, prompting markets to reduce the probability of a September hike to around 50% from 63% previously. The 10-year Treasury yield fell after reaching its highest level since November 2023. Additionally, South Korea posted a strong external balance, with the current account recording a $42.08 billion surplus in July, the second-largest monthly surplus on record and the largest ever recorded for July, with semiconductor-led goods exports remaining robust. However, renewed US plans for targeted semiconductor tariffs linked to companies’ domestic production could weigh on the currency outlook, given the importance of chip exports to Korea’s trade balance.

### 2026-09-04 12:00:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natural Gas Prices Increase — US natural gas prices rose to $2.92 per MMBtu on Friday after sliding 1.5% in the previous session, as investors weighed forecasts for hot weather and improving LNG export activity against ample domestic supplies. Hot weather across the continental US kept cooling demand elevated, supporting gas consumption for power generation. At the same time, average gas flows to the nine major LNG export plants rose to 18.3 bcfd in early September, up from 17.2 bcfd in August, as major facilities in Texas returned to full operations after maintenance. The developments coincided with stronger demand for US LNG in Europe and Asia, where buyers are seeking supplies to refill storage ahead of the winter heating season, amid continued disruptions to LNG flows from the Persian Gulf. Still, abundant domestic supplies are limiting the upside for prices. Gas inventories were 5.2% above their five-year seasonal average as of August 28, while average output in the Lower 48 states remained at record highs.

Corn Holds Near 3-Year High — Corn futures held above $5.10 per bushel, trading near their highest level in more than three years, as concerns over disruptions to Black Sea grain exports added support ahead of the region’s peak corn export season. The peak export window for Black Sea corn begins in October, raising concerns over global supplies as attacks continue to disrupt shipments from Ukraine and Russia. Ukraine accounts for roughly 10-11% of global corn exports, making prolonged disruptions a potential source of tighter supply. Meanwhile, the latest USDA weekly report showed a net reduction of 829,600 metric tons in US corn sales for the 2025/26 marketing year in the week ended August 27, although accumulated exports reached 85.06 million tons, up from 83.49 million a week earlier. Traders are also monitoring US crop prospects ahead of the harvest and await USDA’s updated crop estimates on September 11.

Copper Holds Gains as Supply Risks Persist — Copper futures traded around $6.57 per pound on Friday, holding recent gains as ongoing supply-side challenges continued to support prices. Analysts highlighted a recent export ban from Congo, along with weaker output from major producers Chile and Peru and disruptions linked to El-Nino. Chile’s copper production fell 9.4% year-on-year in July due to severe weather and mine maintenance. China’s refined copper production also declined 3% to 1.1 million tons in June. Meanwhile, tariff uncertainty continues to encourage shipments into the US, pushing Comex inventories to record highs while tightening supplies elsewhere. On the demand side, renewed hostilities between the US and Iran raised concerns that elevated energy prices could weigh on global economic activity and metals demand.

Meanwhile, crude oil prices strengthened amid renewed U.S.–Iran hostilities, raising supply concerns and supporting sentiment. 【碧桂園楊惠妍：必須儘快全面審視並重塑整體經營、開發邏輯與組織架構等】9月3日，碧桂園集團召開月度管理會議，董事會主席楊惠妍剖析了“828新政”下房地產基礎制度的歷史性變革及影響。楊惠妍表示，“828新政”的核心變化在於：從預售制轉向現房銷售、從快週轉轉向重品質、從企業主體信用轉向專案信用。過去依賴快週轉、高槓杆的打法不再適用，行業經營邏輯被徹底改寫。短期陣痛難免，但中長期卻是行業走向成熟與穩健的重要契機。楊惠妍會上提出，在新房地產形勢與政策下，集團必須儘快全面審視並重塑整體經營、開發邏輯與組織架構等，確保在新政策環境下具備敏捷響應能力。碧桂園集團總裁程光煜表示，長期看，新政則將加速風險出清，推動市場走向更健康、可持續的發展；各區域須全面開展資產盤點與策略擬定，集團層面亦將同步調整經營管理邊界。

STI Crosses 5,800 Mark, Tracks Wall Street Higher — Singapore stocks rose 55 points, or 1.0%, to a new record high of 5,803 around noon on Friday, up for the third straight session and tracking an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight amid easing US Treasury yields. The broader index crossed the 5,800 level for the first time, mainly buoyed by gains in communication, non-energy minerals, and finance. However, rising oil prices capped the gains amid heightened inflation concerns and reinforced expectations of an interest rate hike amid escalating Middle East tensions. Traders also anticipated the release of Singapore’s retail sales figures for July and US jobs data due later today. Caution was also building ahead of the release of China’s consumer and producer prices, as well as trade data due next week. Among early gainers were Hong Kong Land (4.2%), Yanzijiang Shipbuilding (3.6%), City Developments (2.4%), OCBC (1.2%), and UOB (1.1%). For the week, the index is heading for a 1.8% rise.

### 2026-09-04 11:20:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybean Futures Hold Above $13 — Soybean futures held above $13 per bushel, near their highest level since December 2023, as fresh Chinese purchases supported demand despite weak US export sales. China has continued buying US soybeans as part of its commitment to purchase 25 million metric tons annually through 2028. The USDA confirmed a 192,000-ton fresh sale to China for 2026/27, following purchases of 202,000 tons a day earlier and 136,000 tons on September 1. US and Chinese officials are also expected to announce measures on agriculture and non-tariff barriers during President Xi Jinping’s visit to Washington this month, raising hopes for lower Chinese tariffs on US farm products. Meanwhile, the latest USDA weekly report showed a net reduction of 94,200 tons in 2025/26 soybean sales for the week ended August 27. However, accumulated exports reached 40.76 million tons, while outstanding sales stood at 1.10 million tons. New-crop demand remained firm, with 2026/27 outstanding sales at 16.28 million tons.

【國家無線電辦公室部署開展衛星網路國內協調第三方機構評估工作】國家無線電辦公室近日印發《關於開展衛星網路國內協調第三方機構評估工作（2026—2027年）的通知》（簡稱《通知》），於2026至2027年度試行開展衛星網路國內協調第三方機構評估工作。《通知》一是明確了評估適用條件，對於協調要求不合理、協調標準不明確、技術分析有爭議、頻率相容結論不明晰等問題，經反覆協調磋商無法達成一致的，協調雙方可以透過第三方機構評估的方式完成協調。二是明確了評估工作程式，包括選取第三方機構、開展評估工作、評估成果運用等。三是明確了評估工作要求，包括客觀公正開展評估、嚴格保密要求、嚴禁違法違規評估、及時反饋意見建議等。下一步，國家無線電辦公室將加強第三方機構評估機制全流程監管，跟蹤機制執行實效，持續完善衛星網路國內協調製度體系，以科學高效的頻軌資源管理支撐我國航天產業高質量發展。

European Gas Prices Fall for 2nd Day — European natural gas prices fell for a second session to around €71.5 per MWh on Friday as investors assessed renewed tensions in the Middle East. The latest flare-up came after a US attack on Iran triggered retaliatory strikes on American bases across the region, sending European gas prices to their highest level in more than three years. Although President Trump indicated that renewed strikes on Tehran would not last too long, tensions remain high around the Strait of Hormuz. The shipping paralysis continues to hamper Gulf LNG supplies to Europe, slowing the pace of inventory replenishment. Europe’s gas inventories for the upcoming winter remain abnormally low, raising the risk of tighter competition and leaving the region vulnerable to price spikes during colder months. Despite the recent decline, European gas prices have climbed more than 6% this week, their fourth consecutive weekly gain.

【兩項國標成功立項 我國深度參與動力電池國際標準制定】作為全球動力電池產業大國，近期，我國依託產業實踐，在國際標準化組織、國際電工委員會等國際組織框架下，積極推進動力電池熱管理、退役電池回收利用等重點領域國際標準研製，為全球動力電池產業協同發展提供標準化支撐。動力電池熱管理系統直接影響電池使用壽命與安全。我國推動在ISO成功立項兩項熱管理領域國際標準，即《電動道路車輛 動力電池包和系統熱管理系統測試方法 第1部分：通用測試》《電動道路車輛 動力電池包及系統熱管理系統測試方法 第2部分：液冷/熱系統》。兩項國際標準旨在確立全球統一的動力電池熱管理系統效能評價方法，為全球動力電池熱管理系統提供有力支撐。動力電池回收利用是產業全生命週期發展的關鍵環節，當前該領域國際標準制定整體尚處於早期階段。我國專家正在積極參與電池效能評估方法和分類、退役電池回收通用指南等全部相關國際標準的制定。我國牽頭提出的《退役電池回收放電指南》IEC國際標準提案已成功立項，現已推進至委員會草案階段。下一步，市場監管總局將引導動力電池生產、使用、回收領域的專業力量更多參與國際標準研究，深化與各國標準化務實交流，助力新能源產業全生命週期高質量、可持續發展。

【行業首家獨立展業 華安證券基金投顧業務轉移至子公司】華安證券公告稱，自2026年9月起，公司基金投資顧問業務已正式轉移至全資子公司華安新興諮詢，原基金投顧服務協議項下的權利和義務由華安新興諮詢承繼。華安新興諮詢成立於1997年12月28日，是國內首批獲得證券投資諮詢業務資格的專業機構。今年上半年，華安證券完成對華安新興諮詢的增資，註冊資本由5000萬元增至1億元，並初步搭建起獨立買方投資顧問業務運營載體，後續買方投顧業務將由子公司華安新興諮詢進行獨立展業。分析人士指出，多數券商選擇以母公司主體開展投顧業務，華安證券透過子公司獨立開展買方投顧 ，在全部券商中尚屬首例。

Japan 10-Year Yield Falls for Second Session — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 2.9% on Friday, declining for a second consecutive session and moving further away from its highest levels since 1996 as the global bond selloff eased. A successful sale of 30-year Japanese government bonds this week also eased market concerns and indicated that investment demand remains firm, with domestic pension funds seeking to increase their JGB allocations in anticipation that the Bank of Japan will accelerate its rate-hiking cycle. Those expectations were reinforced by hawkish remarks from BOJ officials and mounting pressure from the US to support the yen through tighter monetary policy. BOJ board member Hajime Takata raised the possibility of outsized or back-to-back rate hikes, while Governor Kazuo Ueda said policymakers need to pay closer attention to upside price risks. The BOJ is expected to deliver a quarter-point rate hike this month, followed by another increase in December.

### 2026-09-04 10:45:23  #宏觀 #焦點

【事關智慧鎖和養老機器人 我國智慧家居國際標準化建設提速】我國近期深度參與國際標準化組織（ISO）、國際電工委員會（IEC）智慧家居標準化工作，積極推動國際標準立項、研製及釋出落地。其中，我國牽頭制定的《資訊科技 家用電子系統（HES）架構 第5-104部分：資訊裝置資源共享協同服務 基於雲端管理框架的智慧鎖》是全球智慧鎖領域首個由中國牽頭制定的國際標準。我國牽頭制定的另一項國際標準《互聯家居環境中使用的積極輔助生活機器人功能效能準則》是全球首個養老機器人國際標準。擴大智慧消費是提振消費市場、推動民生品質升級的重要抓手。智慧家居作為智慧消費的重要組成部分，正加速從單品智慧向全屋智慧迭代升級。依託國際標準破除互聯瓶頸、共建開放共贏的全球產業生態，已成為行業高質量發展的必然趨勢。《資訊科技 家用電子系統（HES）架構 第5-104部分：資訊裝置資源共享協同服務 基於雲端管理框架的智慧鎖》統一了智慧鎖雲端接入、跨生態互動技術框架，有效破除智慧鎖與全屋智慧家居的互聯壁壘，降低全球產業適配成本，為國產智慧家居產品出海提供權威技術支撐，也提升了我國在國際規則制定中的話語權。《互聯家居環境中使用的積極輔助生活機器人功能效能準則》是我國在智慧家居與智慧養老融合國際標準領域實現的重要突破。該標準明確了家庭場景下養老機器人的功能和效能分類，在監測服務、通訊支援、活動支援等方面分別提出了技術要求，為各類養老機器人的產品設計、製造、測試和認證等提供基準，有效降低適老智慧家居行業適配成本，夯實全球互聯生態基礎。下一步，市場監管總局等部門將聚焦智慧家電、智慧照明、適老智慧裝置等高頻品類，持續推動國內先進技術成果轉化為國際標準，以高水平國際標準賦能產業高質量發展，助力人民群眾打造安全、便捷、智慧的高品質居家生活。

### 2026-09-04 10:38:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Trades Above $1,800 — Platinum futures traded above $1,800 an ounce after climbing from a two-week low, as a weaker US dollar boosted demand for precious metals. The rally followed dovish comments from Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller, who said he would favor keeping interest rates unchanged if inflation continues to ease, prompting investors to scale back bets on a Fed hike this month. Traders now await US jobs data for further clues on the Federal Reserve’s rate outlook. Still, renewed fighting in the Gulf and higher oil prices kept inflation concerns in focus, potentially complicating the Fed’s policy path. Meanwhile, platinum’s industrial demand is expected to rise 9% this year to 2.24 million ounces, according to the World Platinum Investment Council. Valterra Platinum highlighted China’s rapidly expanding hydrogen-truck sector as a potential new demand catalyst, estimating around 6 million ounces of additional global demand if hydrogen trucks reach a 20% share of the global fleet.

Hong Kong Stocks Hit 2-Week High on Tech Rally — The Hang Seng Index rose about 2.2%, or 541 points, to open at 25,751 on Friday, rebounding from the previous session’s losses and reaching its highest level since August 21, supported by a global tech rally and reduced expectations of a September Fed rate hike. Global technology stocks gained overnight after Fed Governor Christopher Waller signalled a preference to keep rates steady if inflation continues to cool, easing concerns over tighter monetary policy and boosting risk appetite. Chinese AI firm Moonshot AI also reportedly confidentially filed for a Hong Kong IPO that could raise around US$3 billion, further lifting sentiment toward the tech sector. Tencent Holdings climbed about 2.2% after an HKEX filing showed it bought back 229,000 shares for HK$100.3 million on September 3. Tech gainers included MiniMax (7.8%), Xiaomi (3.9%), and Meituan (3.7%), while financials rose about 2.4%, led by AIA Group (1.4%), HK Exchanges and Clearing (1.5%), and China Resources Land (2.4%).

【國務院國資委和中國科學院聯合舉辦中央企業未來空間（深海深地）領域人才特訓班】9月2日，由國務院國資委和中國科學院舉辦的中央企業未來空間（深海深地）領域人才特訓班開班。國務院國資委黨委委員、副主任譚作鈞出席開班式並講話。中國科學院副院長、黨組成員何宏平院士作開班致辭。譚作鈞強調，要深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於深海深地產業發展的重要指示精神，落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，切實增強責任感緊迫感，聚焦深海深地產業發展痛點難點，發揮中國科學院基礎研究實力較強、科研力量建制化的優勢，加強院企交流合作，促進中央企業深度連結科研院所優質資源，加快培養高層次領軍人才。要深入研究和把握深海深地產業發展規律，在產業發展形勢上有新認知，在底層技術突破上有新進展，在產學研用協同上有新作為，在人才培養集聚上有新辦法，推動我國深海深地產業高質量發展。此次特訓班由中國船舶、中國五礦、招商局集團、中國鐵建共同承辦，來自22家中央企業、中國科學院12家所屬科研院所共109名學員參訓，國務院國資委和中國科學院有關廳局負責同志、有關中央企業負責同志參加開班式。

Indonesian Shares Set to Close Week Sharply Higher — Indonesia’s IDX Composite edged up 15 points or 0.2% to 6,676 in early Friday trade, holding gains from the prior session and touching its highest level in 3-1/2 months. Sentiment was supported by an overnight Wall Street rally after Fed Governor Waller’s dovish remarks prompted traders to pare back rate-hike bets. Confidence also improved with a smooth leadership transition at Bank Indonesia, as newly inaugurated Governor Destry Damayanti pledged responsive policy to safeguard stability and economic growth. However, upside was tempered by caution ahead of key domestic data due next week, including August forex reserves, consumer sentiment, and July retail sales. Inflation worries also lingered after August figures accelerated amid potential El Niño-related food supply disruptions. Notable movers included Indah Kiat Pulp & Paper (1.7%), Mitra Adiperkasa (1.1%), and Bumi Resources (1.0%). For the week, the market is on track to post solid gains of about 2.7%, reversing prior declines.

US 10-Year Yield Holds Retreat — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note held around 4.76% on Friday after pulling back from three-year highs, as traders reduced bets on a Federal Reserve rate hike this month following dovish remarks from a central bank official. Fed Governor Christopher Waller said he would support keeping rates unchanged if price pressures continue to ease, adding that his next policy decision will be heavily influenced by August inflation data due next week. Traders now see roughly a 50% probability of a September rate hike, down from about 63% a day earlier. Investors are also awaiting Friday’s August jobs report for further clues on the Fed’s policy outlook. Meanwhile, oil prices were on track for a strong weekly gain amid ongoing hostilities in the Middle East and heightened uncertainty over shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, keeping inflationary risks in focus.

【我國牽頭提出的淨零碳城市國際標準在ISO成功立項】我國牽頭提出的《城市和社群可持續發展淨零碳城市 第2部分：原則與要素》國際標準提案，近日在國際標準化組織（ISO）成功立項。該標準聚焦能源、交通、建築、工業、農業、林業、土地利用、迴圈經濟、居住與工作環境等重點領域，從激勵機制、人才隊伍建設、技術支撐、社會文化適配、工具與方法等方面提供參考正規化，旨在為城市制定淨零碳策略提供系統性原則和要素指引，助力城市構建系統化淨零碳發展路徑。如：在能源領域，擬提出可再生能源消納配比規劃指引，協助城市明確清潔能源替代的時間表與路線圖；在交通領域，擬提出新能源充換電及氫能站點佈局指引，推動城市交通用能結構系統性重塑；在建築領域，擬提出綠色建材選型與圍護結構節能實施指引，從源頭降低建築全生命週期碳排放。該標準將作為全球城市碳中和工作落地的首份綜合性技術“操作手冊”，提供一套可衡量、可複製、可驗證的淨零碳行動框架，著力解決當前城市“有目標、無路徑”的治理難題，推動全球城市淨零碳建設從分散“個案”走向通用“正規化”。中國、加拿大、義大利、韓國、葡萄牙、蓋亞那等國專家將聯合開展標準研製工作。

【RBNZ assistant governor says December more likely for next rate hike】RBNZ Assistant Governor Silk said the bank is more likely to wait until December for its next rate increase. The RBNZ raised the OCR at a second consecutive meeting this week; Governor Breman said further hikes may be required but policymakers want time to assess the impact of earlier moves. “We have been clear that, based on the economic outlook, further rate increases may be necessary,” Silk said, adding the timing — October or December — is undecided but the latter is more likely on the current rate path. Swaps price a 31% chance of a 25bp October hike to 3% and a 100% probability of 3% by December. Silk reiterated the RBNZ has no pre-set tightening path.

Investors also continued to monitor sharp moves in the yen after the currency rallied strongly this week on expectations for more aggressive policy tightening by the Bank of Japan, while traders watched for signs of official intervention to support the Japanese currency. 【星塵釋出線上強化學習框架SmoothRL，實現與大模型的非同步推理】近日，星塵智慧（Astribot） 基座模型團隊釋出能非同步執行的線上強化學習框架 SmoothRL（Online Reinforcement Learning During Asynchronous Execution）。解決的是大模型非同步推理成為真實部署常態後，與之適配的online RL到底該從哪些動作裡學。

### 2026-09-04 09:55:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Hits 4-Month High — The Australian dollar broke above $0.72 to a four-month high as expectations of a near-term interest rate hike strengthened, while a weaker US dollar added support. The greenback came under pressure after Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller said he would support leaving rates unchanged if inflation pressures continued to ease, prompting investors to trim bets on a Fed rate hike later this month. Traders now await US jobs data due later today for fresh clues on the Fed’s policy outlook. In Australia, stronger GDP data in the second quarter reinforced expectations that the RBA could resume tightening after three rate hikes this year. Markets now price in a 58% chance of a hike this month, up from 49% before, while a November move is fully priced in. Swaps also imply 40 bps of tightening next year, equivalent to one and a half rate hikes. The hawkish outlook was further reinforced by renewed fighting in the Gulf, which pushed oil prices higher and fueled concerns over inflation.

Yen Heads for Strong Weekly Advance — The Japanese yen traded around 156 per dollar on Friday after surging for two consecutive sessions, putting it on track to gain about 2.5% for its strongest weekly performance since Tokyo and Washington carried out a joint yen-buying operation in late July. There is no confirmation that this week’s rally was driven by official intervention, although traders have speculated that authorities conducted a rate check, which typically precedes intervention. Markets also weighed the prospects for more aggressive interest rate hikes by the Bank of Japan this year following hawkish remarks from central bank officials and mounting pressure from the US to support the yen through tighter monetary policy. Elsewhere, the yen also benefited from broad dollar weakness after Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller said he would support keeping rates steady if price pressures continue to ease.

【State Council work-safety office convenes video meeting to coordinate inspection and cleanup of fake new-energy refueling stations】On Sept. 3 the State Council Work Safety Committee Office held a video meeting to advance inspection and rectification of so-called fake new-energy refueling stations. Local authorities were directed to follow Hubei’s model to leave no gaps and carry out deep, thorough verification of suspected sites. The office ordered strengthened whole-chain joint enforcement, clear assignment of enforcement responsibilities by violation type, unified enforcement standards, tightened discretionary rules and enhanced inter-agency case consultation, maintaining a high-pressure, punish-all approach with penalties proportional to offenses. It also mandated strict implementation of cross-department regulation of refined oil products, regularized sweeps of illegal refueling points (including fake new-energy sites), expanded sources and methods for tip-offs and inspections, and improved clue transfer, information-sharing and joint-handling mechanisms to bolster coordinated supervision, verification and enforcement and prevent rebound.

South Korean Shares Extend Gains — The benchmark KOSPI rose more than 1% to around 6,660 on Friday, extending gains from the previous session, as easing expectations for another US rate hike lifted global risk sentiment. US stocks rallied overnight after Fed Governor Christopher Waller signaled a preference for holding rates steady unless inflation surprises, while the 10-year Treasury yield retreated to 4.77% after reaching its highest level since November 2023. Semiconductor shares also advanced, with Samsung Electronics and SK hynix climbing more than 1% and nearly 3%, respectively. Samsung narrowed its HBM market-share gap with SK hynix in Q2, supporting the outlook for Korea’s key chip sector. Gains were also recorded in SK Square (2.1%), Samsung Electro-Mechanics (2.4%), Samsung SDI (2.5%), and LG Electronics (1.4%). Meanwhile, renewed US plans for targeted semiconductor tariffs linked to companies’ domestic production could limit further gains, with details on rates and exemptions yet to be finalized.

### 2026-09-04 09:30:23  #國際

【我國牽頭的智慧城市人工智慧應用國際標準成功立項】我國牽頭提出的《城市和社群可持續發展 智慧城市運營模型中的人工智慧應用 第2部分：原則與要素》國際標準提案，近日在國際標準化組織（ISO）成功立項。人工智慧正與城市治理深度融合，成為提升城市治理效能的關鍵技術。該標準旨在為智慧城市運營中的人工智慧應用構建系統性治理框架，明確治理體系、實施路徑、協同機制、成效評估等核心要素，著力破解城市“有技術、無規則”困境。在戰略管理層面，標準擬提出風險監督與績效監測指引；在公共服務層面，標準擬提出個性化與普惠服務融合實施指引；在資源管理層面，標準擬提出資料治理與資源協同佈局指引，全方位推動構建包容、透明、負責任的城市AI治理體系。該標準將由中國、美國、英國、法國、阿聯酋、新加坡、馬來西亞、巴西、伊朗等16國專家聯合開展研製。

### 2026-09-04 09:25:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Vietnam #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Vietnam Retail Sales Growth Accelerates in August — Retail sales in Vietnam rose by 14.9% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from a 14.5% increase in July and marking the strongest growth since January 2023. Retail sales of goods, which accounted for around three-quarters of total activity, grew 12.8% year-on-year in the first eight months, while accommodation and catering revenue climbed 16.4% and travel services jumped 17.1%. Sales of gems, precious metals and related products recorded the fastest growth among major retail categories, rising 28.1% over the same period, while sales of other goods and wood and construction materials also posted strong gains. Automobile sales were a notable exception, declining both month-on-month and year-on-year in August. On a monthly basis, total retail sales of goods and consumer services increased 1.6%. Overall, total retail sales reached VND5,235.5 trillion in the first eight months, up 13.3% year-on-year, while growth excluding price effects stood at 7.6%.

Bessent wrote Washington "appreciates its strong and timely stance" and said "the United States and its allies stand together to ensure the Iranian regime cannot use the global financial system to fund its nuclear ambitions, weapons programs and terrorist proxies." He added: "The world is sending a clear message to the Iranian regime: we will spare no effort until cutting off all its remaining financial lifelines." The EU said late last month it would work "closely with the US, other G7 members and international partners to continue to pressure Iran" while seeking de‑escalation and preserving regional stability.

### 2026-09-04 09:18:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Philippines Inflation Slightly Eases in August — The annual inflation rate in the Philippines eased to 6.1% in August 2026 from 6.2% in July, but came above expectations of 6%. Food and non-alcoholic beverage inflation slowed to 4.6% from 5.2% in July, mainly due to a decline in the prices of vegetables, tubers, plantains, cooking bananas and pulses. Inflation for housing and utilities also moderated to 7.9% from 8.2%. On the other hand, transport inflation rose to 13.5% from 11.9%, reflecting renewed fuel price increases in the second half of August. Price growth also increased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.3% vs. 6%), furnishings and household equipment (4.1% vs. 3.9%), and health (5% vs. 4.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices jumped 0.6%, the most since April, following a 0.1% gain in July and exceeding expectations of a 0.5% rise. Meanwhile, core inflation eased to 4.1% from 4.2%, in line with forecasts. Average inflation stood at 5.2% from January to August, remaining above the government’s 2%-4% target range.

### 2026-09-04 09:17:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

Dollar Weakens on Waller Remarks — The dollar index held just below 99 on Friday after sliding sharply in the previous session, as traders scaled back expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month following dovish comments from a central bank official. Fed Governor Christopher Waller said he would support keeping rates unchanged if price pressures continue to ease, adding that his next policy decision will depend heavily on August inflation data due next week. Traders now see roughly a 50% probability of a September rate hike, down from about 63% a day earlier. Investors are also awaiting Friday’s August jobs report for further clues on the Fed’s policy outlook. The dollar also faced pressure from the surging yen as traders watched for signs of intervention while assessing the prospects for more aggressive policy tightening by the Bank of Japan this year. For the week, the dollar index is on track to decline about 0.7%.

### 2026-09-04 09:10:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Philippine Industrial Output Growth at 5-Month Low — Manufacturing production in the Philippines rose by 9.6% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the softest growth since February and slowing from an upwardly revised 13.9% gain in the previous month. The slowdown was largely driven by a sharp moderation in the manufacture of coke and refined petroleum products (47.2% vs 89.3% in June), alongside slower growth in computer, electronic and optical products (17.2% vs 22.8%), food products (2.8% vs 4.4%), and transport equipment (6.5% vs 7.8%). At the same time, output declined for chemicals and chemical products (-19.0% vs -11.8%), machinery and equipment excluding electrical (-16.5% vs -17.0%), and pharmaceutical products (-10.8% vs 5.9%). On the other hand, production accelerated in basic metals (24.2% vs 22.8%), furniture (23.9% vs 66.6%), leather and related products (35.4% vs 21.7%), and apparel (10.4% vs -2.3%). For the January-July period, industrial activity grew by 8.6%, compared with the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-09-04 09:09:13  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【丁薛祥出席第十一屆東方經濟論壇全會並致辭】當地時間2026年9月3日，中共中央政治局常委、國務院副總理丁薛祥在俄羅斯符拉迪沃斯託克出席第十一屆東方經濟論壇全會，並發表題為“深化中俄地區合作 增進兩國人民福祉”的致辭。

丁薛祥表示，東方經濟論壇是各國共商合作、共謀發展的重要平臺。習近平主席先後兩次應普京總統邀請出席論壇並發表重要致辭，系統闡述中方關於東北亞區域合作的主張，在國際社會引起廣泛共鳴。這些年，在兩國元首戰略引領下，中俄“東北－遠東”等地區合作取得豐碩成果。中國穩居俄遠東地區最大貿易伙伴地位，中方積極支援俄遠東地區“一區一港”建設、入駐企業近百家，重點合作專案加速推進，相互免籤政策促進邊境口岸“人貨兩旺”，兩國人民幸福感、獲得感持續提升。

丁薛祥指出，習近平主席同普京總統年內多次會晤，為深化中俄新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係和雙方全方位合作提供戰略指引。中方願同俄方一道，全面落實兩國元首重要共識，推動地區合作向著更深層次、更高水平發展，為中俄關系健康穩定發展注入新的動力。

丁薛祥提出4點建議。一是促進普惠包容發展，讓人民更好共享發展成果。加強共建“一帶一路”同歐亞經濟聯盟建設對接合作，深化東北振興與遠東開發戰略對接，貼近民生需求，加強地區間相互支援，謀劃發展特色產業，讓兩國民眾在發展振興中得到更多實惠。二是營造良好營商環境，便利各類經營主體投資興業。落實好新版《中俄投資合作規劃綱要》和升級版《中俄促進和相互保護投資協定》，及時幫助企業解決關切問題，保持政策連續穩定，為企業發展創造有利條件。三是加強友好往來，夯實民心相通根基。深化文化、旅遊、教育、青年等領域交流合作，開展好“中俄教育年”各項活動，讓兩國人民在友好交往中賡續傳統友誼、更加相知相親。四是維護地區和平穩定，建設人民美好家園。共同捍衛聯合國憲章宗旨和國際公平正義，阻遏挑戰二戰勝利成果和戰後國際秩序的行徑，攜手將本地區建設成為和諧安寧的共同家園。 （外交部）

Australian Shares Edge Higher, Weekly Loss Looms — Australian stocks added 18 points or 0.2% to 9,037 in early trade on Friday, building gains from the prior session following overnight gains on Wall Street as Treasury yields pulled back after Fed Waller said he’d be “inclined to support” holding rates steady barring any surprises in upcoming inflation data. Locally, August services growth was revised higher amid improving confidence that came close to its level before the outbreak of war in the Middle East. Most sectors advanced, led by non-energy minerals, tech, and healthcare. Among major performers were Cochlear Ltd. (2.9%), Perseus Mining (2.1%), and JB Hi-Fi Ltd. (1.5%). The four big banks added between 0.4% and 1.0%. For the week, however, domestic markets are on track to post a decline, down around 0.7% so far following prior gains, dragged by worries that the Reserve Bank may deliver another rate hike at its meeting later this month following stronger-than-expected Q2 GDP data.

【韓國加速政府機構搬離首爾】綜合韓聯社、《京鄉新聞》等韓媒報道，韓國政府3日公佈行政和公共機構搬遷計劃，明年上半年起將首都圈中央行政部門陸續遷往世宗市，以緩解首都圈人口和資源過度集中，提高行政效率。 韓國政府還將對首都圈約350家公共機構進行全面梳理，按照“儘量不留在首都圈”的原則，於今年四季度確定並公佈搬遷名單，2027年正式啟動搬遷。為加快進度，部分機構可先租用民間建築辦公，不必等待新樓建成。政府還將增加搬遷和住房補貼，改善教育、醫療等生活條件，幫助機構工作人員及其家屬在遷入地長期定居。 韓國國務總理韓聖淑表示，首都圈“一極化”和國土發展失衡，是韓國諸多經濟社會問題的重要根源，均衡發展已不是可選項，而是關係國家可持續發展的“生存戰略”。此次搬遷也旨在解決中央部門分散辦公造成的行政低效。目前韓國多數中央部門已遷至世宗市，但法務部等仍留在首爾，國會和總統辦公室也主要設在首爾。（環球時報）

Kiwi Dollar Advances as Greenback Weakens — The New Zealand dollar rose to around $0.589 on Friday, extending gains from the previous session as the US dollar came under pressure following comments from Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller. Waller said he would support leaving interest rates unchanged if inflation pressures continued to ease, prompting investors to trim bets on a Fed rate hike later this month. Traders are now looking to US jobs data due later today for fresh clues on the Fed’s policy outlook. On the domestic policy front, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised interest rates for a second consecutive meeting earlier this week but signalled a less aggressive path for further tightening. This has led traders to increasingly expect the central bank to hold rates at its next meeting in October, limiting the kiwi’s upside. Despite its recent gains, the kiwi is down 0.5% this week, on track for a second straight weekly loss.

【厄爾尼諾衝擊秘魯漁業 牽動國際市場】厄爾尼諾現象持續影響秘魯近海海域。海水溫度異常升高，正在改變鯷魚的分佈和生存環境，給秘魯傳統漁業帶來明顯衝擊。作為全球重要的魚粉和魚油生產國，秘魯鯷魚供應的變化，不僅影響當地漁業公司及漁民，也正在推高國際市場相關產品的價格。今年受厄爾尼諾現象影響，秘魯近海海洋環境發生變化，鯷魚的分佈和可捕撈量受到明顯影響。根據原定計劃，今年第一捕撈季的鯷魚配額為191萬噸，但截至6月初，實際捕撈量僅約47萬噸，完成率不足四分之一。受海洋條件惡化及鯷魚進入繁殖期等因素疊加影響，秘魯生產部於6月11日正式宣佈暫停中北部海域的鯷魚捕撈作業。（央視）

Japanese Shares Rise as Fed Hike Bets Ease — The Nikkei 225 Index rose 0.6% to above 64,500 on Friday, snapping a four-day losing streak as traders scaled back expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Fed Governor Christopher Waller said he would support keeping rates unchanged if price pressures continue to ease. Meanwhile, markets are watching for a potential rate increase by the Bank of Japan at its September meeting to contain inflationary pressures. Investors also continued to monitor sharp movements in the yen after the currency gained as much as 3% over two sessions. Technology stocks led the rebound, with notable gains from Kioxia Holdings (1.8%), SoftBank Group (5.8%), and Fujikura (1.2%). In contrast, financial and consumer stocks declined, including Mitsubishi UFJ (-1.1%) and Toyota Motor (-0.6%). Still, the Nikkei is on track to fall more than 2% for the week as elevated oil prices and a recent rise in global bond yields weighed on equities during the period.

【外國經營者在加速離開日本】隨著日本政府收緊外國人經營企業的簽證條件，在日本開公司、經營餐飲店等業務的外國人正在加速離開日本。據《日本經濟新聞》9月2日報道，2026年上半年，共有953名持“經營管理”在留資格的外國人在沒有辦理再入境手續的情況下離開日本，是上年同期的3.9倍。 外國經營者離日增加的趨勢從去年底開始變得明顯。2025年秋季以前，未辦理再入境所需手續便離開日本的人數通常只有幾十人，同年12月增至114人，今年以來進一步達到每月100至200多人。 這些變化的背後，是日本政府從2025年10月起大幅收緊經營管理在留資格。根據日本出入國在留管理廳公佈的新規，要求外國人在日本經營企業的註冊資本從原來的500萬日元（100日元約合4.3元人民幣）提高到3000萬日元以上；申請人還須擁有相關領域的碩士等學位，或具有3年以上經營或管理經驗。同時必須僱用至少1名符合條件的全職員工。（環球時報）

【中信證券：中東局勢再度升溫，助推海外風險敘事】中信證券研報指出，美伊衝突延宕已逾半年，近期局勢再次出現升溫跡象。探究升溫原因，我們認為，伊朗內部強硬派勢力上升疊加海峽作為談判籌碼存在有效期，因此有動力在威懾能力弱化前採取進攻性策略，換取美國在更多戰略問題上讓步。展望後續局勢，全面戰爭仍非基準情景，但低烈度衝突反覆和誤判升級的機率正在上升。在美伊僵局難解的情況下，即使伊朗與阿曼達成通航協議，也難以化解海峽通航風險。未來需觀察到美伊任一方內部政治壓力產生明顯變化的訊號，才可能觸發實質性讓步，例如共和黨參議院選情告急，或伊朗經濟壓力引發內部動亂等。剖析市場影響，當前國際原油期貨定價並未充分反映中東局勢的挑戰以及成品油短缺的風險。中東局勢已成為海外諸多風險敘事的助推劑，將階段性加劇海外貨幣政策兩難與債務困局敘事，預期能否反轉仍需等待上述的關鍵訊號。

Silver Holds Gain on Waller Remarks — Silver traded near $67 an ounce on Friday after rising for two consecutive sessions, as dovish comments from Federal Reserve Governor Christopher Waller led markets to scale back expectations for a September rate hike. Waller said he would favor keeping rates unchanged if price pressures continue to ease. Traders now see roughly a 50% probability of a September rate hike, down from about 63% a day earlier. Investors are also awaiting Friday’s payrolls report and next week’s inflation data for additional clues on the Fed’s policy outlook. The US dollar and Treasury yields fell sharply following Waller’s remarks, supporting precious metals. Meanwhile, oil prices were on track for a strong weekly gain amid ongoing hostilities in the Middle East and heightened uncertainty over shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, keeping inflationary risks in focus.

【加拿大總理：貿易協議應具備穩定性與可信度】加拿大與美國的貿易談判日前宣告破裂。加拿大總理卡尼3日在安大略省視察當地製造業時表示，只要美方做好準備，加方隨時願意坐下來談判，但任何協議都必須具備穩定性與可信度。針對美方稱加拿大因國內政治因素退出談判，卡尼指出，美方此前在談判中擺出“要麼聽我的，要麼免談”的姿態，直到最後一刻仍在爭執部分議題，不過近期立場有所軟化。卡尼重申，加拿大在談判中一直力爭取消不合理關稅，對美方方案及其可持續性均不滿意。加方此前已宣佈自9月8日起對美國多行業實施反制關稅措施。卡尼也表示，一份符合加拿大工人、家庭和企業利益的協議同樣符合美方利益，只要美方做好準備，加方隨時願意坐下來談判，但任何協定都必須具備穩定性與可信度。卡尼當天還宣佈，加拿大政府將投入47億加元，在加本土為國有企業維亞鐵路生產客運車廂。這也是四十年來，該鐵路公司的車廂首次實現國內製造。

【物價上漲 日本家庭支出連續第八個月下滑】受日用品成本走高促使消費者削減非必需消費影響，日本家庭支出連續第八個月出現回落。日本總務省週五公佈資料，經通脹調整後的7月家庭支出同比下降3.6%，經濟學家此前預期為下降1.6%；經季節調整後，家庭支出較6月上漲0.5%。住房修繕、租金以及汽車相關開支下滑拖累整體指數走低。儘管政府出臺舉措壓制能源成本，但食品等日用品物價持續走高，家庭仍承壓，消費支出因此長期走弱。7月生鮮食品價格同比上漲7%，加工食品漲幅回落至3%。根據日本帝國資料庫公司公佈，7月計劃漲價的商品數量同比上升近22%，該趨勢還將延續。受中東衝突推高原油、石腦油價格以及日元貶值影響，預計8月調價商品數量將大增83%，9月漲價潮還會再度來襲。

【紐約市禁高中以下學生用AI 有網民認為新規涉嫌“一刀切”】本月2日，美國紐約市頒佈重磅“AI禁令”，擬大範圍限制該市中小學以及學前教育階段學生接觸生成式人工智慧（AI）應用程式。 美國有線電視新聞網9月3日報道稱，紐約新頒佈的“AI禁令”涵蓋該市從2歲學前教育至中學8年級的絕大多數學生群體，是美國迄今為止針對學校實施的覆蓋面最廣的一道限制政策，預計有60萬名學生將受到直接影響。 值得注意的是，高中生群體雖不在“AI禁令”的限制範圍內，但也只能使用功能有限的AI工具，其中不包括如ChatGPT以及Claude等主流互動式人工智慧大語言模型。紐約市的新舉措也引爆網路，不少網民認為新規涉嫌“一刀切”。芝加哥公立學區前負責人讓-克勞德·布里扎德表示，紐約與洛杉磯兩大學區長期以來都是全美教育創新的“燈塔”，但在AI的問題上卻“找錯了方向”。 （環球時報）

US Futures Steady Ahead of August Jobs Report — US stock futures were little changed on Friday as investors awaited the closely watched August payrolls report for fresh clues about the health of the labor market and the outlook for monetary policy. 【天然橡膠價格創近兩年新高 輪胎企業密集釋出漲價通知】下半年以來，天然橡膠市場延續強勢格局。9月初，國內天然橡膠現貨價格站上17900元/噸，較年初上漲約14%，創近兩年高位。截至9月3日，滬膠期貨主力合約一度突破18800元/噸。在原材料成本持續攀升的背景下，下游輪胎行業掀起新一輪漲價潮，相關上市公司業績分化。此輪橡膠漲價並非單一因素驅動。天然橡膠生產國協會（ANRPC）預測，2026年全球天然橡膠產量約1532萬噸，消費量達1560萬噸，供需缺口約28萬噸，且較2025年有所擴大。主產國方面，泰國橡膠樹齡老化、印尼葉片脫落病蔓延、越南產量預期下滑，疊加厄爾尼諾現象導致產區乾旱，供給端收縮態勢明顯。與此同時，受地緣政治影響，合成橡膠原料丁二烯供應緊張，價格創歷史新高，下游企業被迫轉向天然橡膠替代採購，進一步推升天然橡膠需求。成本壓力迅速向產業鏈下游傳導。2026年以來，包括米其林、普利司通、中策橡膠、賽輪輪胎在內的眾多輪胎企業密集釋出漲價通知，漲幅普遍在2%至5%。外資品牌與國內龍頭同步調價，顯示此輪成本衝擊具有行業普遍性。

【涉擬IPO企業投訴舉報中 軟體侵權居高不下】“籌備上市期間，我們收到過一些軟體商的侵權提示函。有的軟體，我們公司其實已經購買了正版的，但對方還是會向監管部門投訴，要求我們增加購買套數。”楊柳是一家半導體企業的證券事務代理，她告訴證券時報記者，公司啟動IPO（首次上市募資）後就被一些軟體商“盯”上，令公司頗為困擾。對於許多企業而言，IPO是一場與時間的賽跑，任何“橫生的枝節”都可能增加成本，甚至導致上市失敗。但在這個敏感期，軟體維權正成為讓不少企業頗感頭疼的問題。證券時報記者從接近監管的人士處瞭解到，涉擬IPO企業投訴舉報中，軟體侵權一直居高不下，在部分市場板塊甚至高達八成。近年來，隨著國家層面對智慧財產權的保護持續增強，司法層面大力打擊相關的侵權行為，軟體權利人的維權意識與維權手段也同步升級，軟體維權與上市稽核的碰撞，正變得越來越頻繁。

Brent Set for Sharp Weekly Rise — Brent crude climbed toward $96 per barrel on Friday and was on track for a gain of around 9% this week, underpinned by renewed hostilities in the Middle East and growing uncertainty surrounding shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. The US carried out fresh strikes against Iran this week following roughly a month of relative calm, prompting Tehran to retaliate by targeting American bases in the region and vessels passing through Hormuz. Six commodity vessels transited the strait on Wednesday, down from 11 on Tuesday and compared with a 10-day average of nearly 13. Damage to refineries in the Middle East and Russia, coupled with limited capacity elsewhere to compensate for supply disruptions, is also expected to keep global fuel prices elevated into next year. Meanwhile, US diesel prices reached their highest level since mid-2022 this week, while inventories in Europe remain well below seasonal norms.

### 2026-09-04 07:10:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Current Account #Importance 1

South Korea Current Account Surplus Narrows in July — South Korea’s current account surplus narrowed to USD 42.08 billion in July 2026, down from a record high of USD 49.73 billion in June. The goods account surplus fell to USD 40.43 billion in July from USD 47.89 billion in June, with exports soaring 65.3% yoy to USD 100.45 billion, while imports rose 21.7% to USD 60.02 billion. Exports surged, boosted by strong growth in semiconductor exports amid the global AI boom. The primary income account surplus increased to USD 4.35 billion from USD 3.27 billion, driven by higher income on equity. Additionally, the secondary income deficit widened to USD 0.73 billion from USD 0.14 billion, reflecting higher secondary income payments. Meanwhile, the services account deficit rose to USD 1.97 billion from USD 1.209 billion, due to larger deficits in manufacturing services and the use of intellectual property. For the 1st seven months of 2026, the current account surplus jumped to USD 233.09 billion from USD 59.82 billion in the same period of 2025.

New Zealand Equities Head for Weekly Gain — New Zealand stocks climbed 94 points, or 0.7%, to 13,940 in Friday morning trade, after retreating in the previous session, tracking a rise on Wall Street overnight due to easing Treasury yields. The gain was mainly supported by advances in financials, energy, materials, utilities, and healthcare. However, traders anticipated US jobless rate data due later today, China’s consumer and producer data, as well as trade figures for August due next week. Domestically, manufacturing PMI data will also be announced next week, after factory activity softened in July. Among early gainers were Infratil (2.4%), Briscoe Group (1.4%), Contact Energy (1.4%), EBOS Group (0.8%), Auckland International Airport (0.7%), A2 Milk (0.6%), Fisher & Paykel (0.5%), and Freightways Group (0.5%). For the week, the index is heading for a 1.2% rise, after falling in the previous month.

Ibovespa Closes Flat After 11-Session Rally — The Ibovespa closed flat at 185,188 on Thursday, ending an 11-session winning streak. The index had climbed nearly 2% at the open before investors took profits. The rally had been supported by recent election polls showing President Lula narrowing his lead over Flávio Bolsonaro, who is viewed by markets as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated yields and weak business activity. A Datafolha poll was due later Thursday. Meanwhile, although the latest economic activity data pointed to a second consecutive contraction in private-sector activity, the downturn was less severe than in the previous month. Services activity rebounded slightly as demand conditions showed signs of stabilisation. Major banks helped offset losses, with Itaú up 1.2%, Santander gaining 1.7%, and Banco do Brasil adding 0.8%. Embraer rose 1.5% after announcing a cybersecurity business combination with SPX Capital. CSN surged 6.7% after announcing a change of CEO. Vale fell 2.9% and Petrobras declined 1.3%.

【美國副總統萬斯淡化伊朗衝突規模 稱不會稱其為戰爭】美國副總統JD·萬斯淡化了伊朗衝突規模，稱他不會將其描述為“一場戰爭”。眼下美伊衝突已進入第七個月，而中東地區的軍事打擊在過去幾天升級。“我不會稱之為戰爭，”萬斯週四在白宮新聞釋出會上表示。“目前並沒有正在進行的交火。”“我承認，有些地方確實出現了升級，”萬斯表示。“大規模作戰行動持續了約六週。”萬斯週四代替前白宮新聞秘書萊維特主持記者會時還重申了政府的觀點，稱伊朗“對霍爾木茲海峽的控制實際上已經不復存在，這一資產的價值在下降。”在被問及汽油價格何時會回落至每加侖約3美元時，萬斯拒絕作出預測，但他表示，美國保護商業航運的行動幫助約1500萬桶原油於週三經霍爾木茲海峽出口。目前，美國全國汽油零售均價仍維持在每加侖4美元以上。萬斯將高油價完全歸咎於伊朗對霍爾木茲海峽商船的襲擊。他說：“現實情況是，目前汽油價格如此之高，根本原因在於伊朗在向商業船隻開火。”

TSX Rises on Lower Fed Rate Hike Bets — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 1.5% to close at 36,633 s as bets on a US Fed rate hike in September eased. US Fed Governor Waller said that he was inclined to be patient on monetary policy while monitoring whether price pressures continue to ease. Following his comments, markets trimmed expectations for a Fed rate hike, while both Canadian and US government bond yields retreated from their 2025 highs. Banks advanced, with RBC up 1.6% and both BMO and CIBC gaining 1.8%. Gold prices rose, driving miners higher. Agnico Eagle jumped 5%, Barrick gained 3.1%, and WPM added 3.7%. Technology stocks posted sharp gains amid a rally in US hyperscalers. Shopify rose 2.3%, while Celestica soared 11.3% as hyperscaler capital expenditure continued to fuel rapid expansion in high-performance AI computing and enterprise hardware. Meanwhile, despite the BoC keeping rates unchanged on Wednesday, Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated.

【Volkswagen to cut about 50,000 jobs, close four European plants in major restructuring】Volkswagen Group will launch a comprehensive restructuring to cut roughly 50,000 jobs and close four plants, calling it the most far-reaching transformation in the group's history. The move follows protracted talks with management, unions and the Lower Saxony state government. The supervisory board confirmed about 500,000 units of excess production capacity in Europe. Emden, Zwickau, Hanover and Neckarsulm currently have no competitive follow-on production projects over the next 5–8 years; the group said it is exploring alternative uses. The cuts will include management positions. CEO Oliver Blume had earlier proposed a larger package of up to 100,000 job cuts and up to four plant closures.

### 2026-09-04 04:08:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Gain as Treasury Yields Retreat — US stock indices closed higher on Thursday as bond yields halted their recent surge, supporting credit-sensitive sectors. The S&P 500 rose 1.1%, the Dow gained 624 points, and the Nasdaq advanced 1.2%. Fed Governor Waller signaled that a rate hike would not be warranted if underlying inflation continues to slow, marking a shift from earlier remarks highlighting inflation risks. Treasury yields subsequently eased. Credit-sensitive software stocks advanced, with Microsoft up 2.7%, Meta rising 3%, Oracle jumping 5.7%, and Palantir soaring 7.7%. Chipmakers were mixed, with Nvidia and Intel gaining 1.8% each, while Broadcom fell 2.7% despite reporting 221% annual revenue growth in its latest fiscal quarter. Hewlett Packard Enterprise added 5% despite results falling short of expectations. Gains among financial heavyweights also supported the Dow, with JPMorgan up 1.6%, Goldman Sachs gaining 3.3%, and Morgan Stanley adding 2.5%.

### 2026-09-04 03:04:47  #公司

【OpenAI釋出GPT-6 Astra模型 稱其或接近實現AGI】OpenAI週四開始釋出新旗艦模型GPT-6 Astra，並稱其在金融建模、工程設計等商業任務領域取得重大進展。該模型初期僅向部分機構開放，包括參與OpenAI Daybreak Access網路安全專案的組織，隨後將向ChatGPT Plus、Pro、Business和Enterprise使用者，以及透過API和亞馬遜雲服務使用的使用者開放。OpenAI表示，GPT-6 Astra在計算機操作、軟體程式設計、專業工作等方面取得顯著提升，並在FrontierMath Tier 1數學測試、ARC-AGI 3推理測試以及ExploitBench網路安全測試中達到最高水平。OpenAI聯合創始人兼總裁格雷格·布洛克曼表示，通用人工智慧（AGI）的實現時間一直存在模糊性，但“幾年後回看”，這一里程碑“可能就在這一時期、就在這個模型附近”。GPT-6 Astra定價為每百萬輸入詞元10美元、輸出詞元50美元，是此前旗艦模型GPT-5.6 Sol價格的2.5倍。OpenAI表示，未來AI行業可能轉向按任務而非按詞元收費。布洛克曼在釋出會上表示：“歡迎來到AGI時代。”

### 2026-09-04 02:43:54  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #META.O #Impact bullish

【Zuckerberg told Trump he opposes a national AI regulator】Business Insider reported Meta CEO Mark Zuckerberg told President Trump last month he opposed plans for a national AI regulator. Sources said Trump phoned Zuckerberg the week of Aug. 17 to discuss a White House consideration of a FINRA-style independent body, a proposal from DeepMind CEO Demis Hassabis to subject advanced AI models to review and risk testing. Zuckerberg said any regulator should reflect the Trump administration’s relatively permissive approach to AI oversight but did not press Trump to change course. The White House said it is weighing the plan while seeking to balance AI innovation and safety and is also evaluating an industry-led self-regulatory option. Zuckerberg has warned that overly strict rules could slow model releases and harm U.S. competitiveness in AI.

Uruguay Inflation Edges Higher in August — Uruguay’s annual inflation rate edged up to 4.55% in August 2026 from 4.27% in June. The largest price increases were recorded in transportation (7.06%), education services (7.02%), restaurants and accommodation services (6.78%), and health (6.52%). Housing-related costs also rose significantly, by 5.13%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages increased 3.14%, while alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics rose 2.98%. In contrast, clothing and footwear fell 6.21%, making it the only category to record deflation. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.24%, bringing year-to-date inflation to 3.65%.
